# Decennium Descent — Bestiary of Aethel
*Director monster development encyclopedia. 707 indexed entries. 2026-07-31.*
Each entry provides ecology notes for Directors, then a combat line derived from the portable Excel bestiary and companion ingest.
Where Combat Provenance or companion flags say draft/sandbox, announce that at the table. Excel remains the editable source; re-ingest after changes.
Gate Guardians are called out explicitly. Prefer their live names; do not revive retired cosmology combat aliases.
Drops and crafting materials live on related Excel sheets—summarize at the table from the workbook when harvest matters.
This book is a development lore-and-stat volume for Decennium Descent. Aethel-specific creatures and naming sit beside compatibility/prototype placeholders used to stress-test full-floor coverage; those placeholders are not final commercial publication copy. See `../development/BESTIARY_PUBLICATION_STATUS.md`.

- Ashthirst the Hollow

## Ecologies of Aethel

### On Aberrations
Born where the Spiral thins and difference curdles into wrong geometry. Directors should emphasize incomprehension and environmental wrongness.

### On Beasts
Natural or dungeon-twisted animals. Use them as resource pressure and travel threats as often as set-piece fights.

### On Celestials
Agents or echoes of higher harmony. Rarely simple allies—Pact politics stain even radiant motives.

### On Constructs
Forged purpose. In the Anvil tiers, constructs are labor, weapons, and evidence of older engineering.

### On Dragons
Apex predators of ambition. Their hoards seed surface wars for generations.

### On Elementals
Concentrated states of matter and energy along Spiral folds. Terrain is half the encounter.

### On Feys
Adjacent-fold beings. Bargains cost more than steel.

### On Fiends
Chaos-Realm lineages—devils of bargained discord and demons of uncontrolled fracture. Their eternal war feeds dissonance the Pact requires.

### On Giants
Scale and siege. Use them to break camps and force Expeditions to choose what to save.

### On Humanoids
Peoples of Aethel and the upper floors. Politics matter; slaughter has reputational cost.

### On Monstrositys
Twisted life that should not breed true—until it does.

### On Oozes
Digestive hazards. Punish poor watch rotations and sealed corridors.

### On Plants
Bloomdeep and verdant hells. Area control and attrition.

### On Undeads
History that refuses silence. Necrotic pressure tests Support teams.

### Ashthirst the Hollow
Medium Aberration, Unaligned. Challenge 12 (155000 XP). Zone: Aethel Jungles.
Ashthirst the Hollow is a Gate Guardian—a seal-intelligence woven into the Heartcell's public face as Crucible myth. Directors should stage this foe as a tier threshold with campaign consequences: failed gates stall Opening progress; victories reactivate seal machinery and scatter loot-as-bait into Aethel. Zone association: Aethel Jungles. Challenge context 12. Born where the Spiral thins and difference curdles into wrong geometry. Directors should emphasize incomprehension and environmental wrongness. When running Ashthirst the Hollow, telegraph the tier's principle before the fight and ensure Support/Research teams have off-map work so the Combat team is not the only story in the room.
**Armor 16; Hit Points 195; Speed 40 ft., fly 60 ft. (hover).**
- Attack I: Ash Claw — +8 to hit — 2d8+4 necrotic
- Multiattack: 2
- Save DC: 16
- Traits: A hollow specter of ash and wind, guardian of Floor 30 in the Fields of Whispering Grasses. It thirsts for the life force of the living.
*Draft/sandbox combat block.*
- Provenance: draft: companion-authored (preserved)

### Lumen of the Shattered Thread
Large Undead, True Neutral. Challenge 30 (155000 XP). Zone: Aethel Caves.
Lumen of the Shattered Thread is a Gate Guardian—a seal-intelligence woven into the Heartcell's public face as Crucible myth. Directors should stage this foe as a tier threshold with campaign consequences: failed gates stall Opening progress; victories reactivate seal machinery and scatter loot-as-bait into Aethel. Zone association: Aethel Caves. Challenge context 30. History that refuses silence. Necrotic pressure tests Support teams. When running Lumen of the Shattered Thread, telegraph the tier's principle before the fight and ensure Support/Research teams have off-map work so the Combat team is not the only story in the room.
**Armor 19; Hit Points 255; Speed 40 ft., fly 80 ft..**
- Attack I: Thread Lance — +10 to hit — 3d8+5 radiant
- Multiattack: 2
- Save DC: 18
- Traits: A radiant being of pure elemental energy, guardian of Floor 50 in the Convergence of the Four Winds. Its form shifts between the four elements.
*Draft/sandbox combat block.*
- Provenance: draft: companion-authored (preserved)

### The Bloom That Hungers
Gargantuan Plant, Chaotic Evil. Challenge 1/8 (155000 XP). Zone: Planar Crossroads.
The Bloom That Hungers is a Gate Guardian—a seal-intelligence woven into the Heartcell's public face as Crucible myth. Directors should stage this foe as a tier threshold with campaign consequences: failed gates stall Opening progress; victories reactivate seal machinery and scatter loot-as-bait into Aethel. Zone association: Planar Crossroads. Challenge context 1/8. Bloomdeep and verdant hells. Area control and attrition. When running The Bloom That Hungers, telegraph the tier's principle before the fight and ensure Support/Research teams have off-map work so the Combat team is not the only story in the room.
**Armor 15; Hit Points 112; Speed 20 ft., burrow 30 ft..**
- Attack I: Vine Maw — +6 to hit — 2d8+4 piercing
- Save DC: 14
- Traits: A massive carnivorous plant that serves as the gate guardian of Floor 10 in the Echoing Grotto. Its writhing vines and snapping maw hunger for flesh.
*Draft/sandbox combat block.*
- Provenance: draft: companion-authored (preserved)
